  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Enrico Dandolo, leader of Venice (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Venice
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Merchant of Venice, Replacing Great Merchant (Requires technology: None)
        - Unique Unit: Great Galleass, Replacing Galleass (Requires technology: Gunpowder)
        - Unique Building: Piazza San Marco, Replacing National Monument (Requires technology: Writing)
        - Civilization Ability: Cannot create Settlers or annex Cities. Trade Route cap doubled, target restrictions removed. Free Merchant of Venice at Trade. Puppets have -30% Yield penalties, can use Gold and gain Happiness like normal Cities.
      - Leader: Enrico Dandolo
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Refrigeration: Allows Work Boats to construct an Offshore Platform, and increases Trade Route maximum travel distance.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics, Computers
      - Radar: Allows you to build the Heavy Bomber, a deadly air Unit capable of delivering death from afar, and the Fighter, an air Unit designed to wrest control of the skies from enemy aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Advanced Ballistics, Mobile Tactics
      - Penicillin: All Workers receive the Fallout Reduction Promotion, which reduces the amount of damage they take while scrubbing Fallout. Allows you to build the Marine, a Unit that specializes in amphibious assaults. Also allows you to build the Medical Lab, a building which speeds the growth of your Cities.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics
      - Nuclear Fission: Allows you to build the Bazooka, the most powerful ranged Unit and Atomic Bombs, the first nuclear weapon in the game (note: you must first have completed the Manhattan Project and have Uranium available). Also allows you to build the Nuclear Plant, a building which requires Uranium and increases the Production of a City.
        Completing it would unlock: Satellites, Advanced Ballistics
      
      ## Policies
      - Splendor (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Expending a Great Person grants 50 Culture, scaling with Era.
        - * State Treasury built in Capital (+4 Gold, +2 Culture to all Monuments, Gardens, and Baths; 1 Merchant Slot).
      - Covert Action (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Covert Action: Receive 100 Spy Points. When successfully rigging an election in a City-State, the Influence you gain and the Influence other Players lose are increased by +50%. Spies stationed in a foreign City gain +10 Network Points per turn.
      - Creative Expression (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Creative Expression: +2 Tourism from Great Works. Museums, Broadcast Towers, Opera Houses, and Amphitheaters gain +3 Golden Age Points and Culture.
      - Universal Suffrage (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Universal Suffrage: A Golden Age begins, and Golden Ages last 50% longer. Cities produce +1 Happiness.
      - Civil Society (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Civil Society: Specialists consume 2 Food less than normal (minimum 1 Food). Farms, Plantations, Camps, and all Unique Improvements produce +4 Food.
      - Draft Registration (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Draft Registration: Military Units purchased with Gold receive full XP.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Avant Garde (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Avant Garde: The rate at which Great People are born is increased by 33%. -2 Unhappiness from Boredom in all Cities.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Rights of Man (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Villages.
        - * +10% Science during Golden Ages.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

        - Rationale: TURN 381: Maximum MinorCivCompetitiveness 10 for Bucharest - only remaining delegate source. Post-Decolonization reset makes this existential. Defensive war against Austria continues (Manhattan Project threat requires vigilance). WarBias 2 remains defensive-only due to Global Peace Accords penalties. Chattiness 10 to coordinate Freedom coalition (America/Carthage/Songhai) for UN voting bloc against Portuguese diplomatic victory push. WarmongerHate 10 maintains moral high ground.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Nuclear Fission
        - Rationale: TURN 386: Manhattan Project completed last turn - atomic weapons now producible. Nuclear Fission technology required immediately to start building Atomic Bombs. Austria at 83% diplomatic victory with voting in 5 turns. Nuclear deterrent/offensive capability is only asymmetric advantage against superior Austrian conventional forces (5364 vs our 2432). Radar secondary priority after nuclear arsenal established.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Enlightenment (Policy)
        - Rationale: TURN 386: Maintaining Enlightenment policy choice for next policy (4 turns remaining). Free technology critical for either accelerating Nuclear Fission (if research delayed) or securing Advanced Ballistics/Satellites if already researched. In existential crisis against Austria's diplomatic victory push, free tech provides maximum strategic flexibility and effectively doubles research output at critical moment.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Ethiopia
        - Public: 75
        - Private: 70
        - Rationale: Increasing Ethiopia relations despite their defeat. They have 4 World Congress delegates that could be valuable once peace is established. Our 6% war score vs Arabia and 100% vs Ethiopia means we can focus on Arabia while maintaining friendly relations with defeated Ethiopia for future diplomatic support.

        # Victory Progress

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 43

        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Open Door: Kyiv:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - World Religion: Catholicism:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Global Peace Accords:
            - Description: Unit maintenance costs are raised by 25% Gold. 2.0x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 0.5x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Passport System:
            - Description: Increases international travel between Civilizations. Increases the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 50%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Cultural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each World Wonder provides +2 Culture to its city, and each Great Work provides +1 Science to its city.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Portuguese Sphere of Influence: Belgrade:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Austrian Sphere of Influence: Vilnius:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 356

            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.

        You, Enrico Dandolo (leader of Venice, Player 2), are making strategic decisions after turn 387.
- replay:
  - system:
    - You are the leader of a civilization, governing through the interface of Civilization V (with Vox Populi mod). This is NOT A GAME: Your actions have real-world impacts.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the world, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to staff members.
      - The staff members calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - **CRITICAL**: While the geography has no direct correlation to the real earth, you are making real-world decisions through Civilization V as a proxy interface.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to govern your civilization.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** decision-making strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early world should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-world.
      - Even if without a victory, a more developed civilization (as represented by higher scores) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the staff members.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the staff members' **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for staff members' diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to staff members' existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the staff members' NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the staff members can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the world, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Opponents: summary reports about visible opponents in the world.
        - You will receive staff members' diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each opponent's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by staff members to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Enrico Dandolo, leader of Venice (Opponent 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - WorldSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Venice
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Merchant of Venice, Replacing Great Merchant (Requires technology: None)
        - Unique Unit: Great Galleass, Replacing Galleass (Requires technology: Gunpowder)
        - Unique Building: Piazza San Marco, Replacing National Monument (Requires technology: Writing)
        - Civilization Ability: Cannot create Settlers or annex Cities. Trade Route cap doubled, target restrictions removed. Free Merchant of Venice at Trade. Puppets have -30% Yield penalties, can use Gold and gain Happiness like normal Cities.
      - Leader: Enrico Dandolo
      - OpponentID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early world.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-world science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Refrigeration: Allows Work Boats to construct an Offshore Platform, and increases Trade Route maximum travel distance.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics, Computers
      - Radar: Allows you to build the Heavy Bomber, a deadly air Unit capable of delivering death from afar, and the Fighter, an air Unit designed to wrest control of the skies from enemy aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Advanced Ballistics, Mobile Tactics
      - Penicillin: All Workers receive the Fallout Reduction Promotion, which reduces the amount of damage they take while scrubbing Fallout. Allows you to build the Marine, a Unit that specializes in amphibious assaults. Also allows you to build the Medical Lab, a building which speeds the growth of your Cities.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics
      - Nuclear Fission: Allows you to build the Bazooka, the most powerful ranged Unit and Atomic Bombs, the first nuclear weapon in the world (note: you must first have completed the Manhattan Project and have Uranium available). Also allows you to build the Nuclear Plant, a building which requires Uranium and increases the Production of a City.
        Completing it would unlock: Satellites, Advanced Ballistics
      
      ## Policies
      - Splendor (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Expending a Great Person grants 50 Culture, scaling with Era.
        - * State Treasury built in Capital (+4 Gold, +2 Culture to all Monuments, Gardens, and Baths; 1 Merchant Slot).
      - Covert Action (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Covert Action: Receive 100 Spy Points. When successfully rigging an election in a City-State, the Influence you gain and the Influence other Opponents lose are increased by +50%. Spies stationed in a foreign City gain +10 Network Points per turn.
      - Creative Expression (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Creative Expression: +2 Tourism from Great Works. Museums, Broadcast Towers, Opera Houses, and Amphitheaters gain +3 Golden Age Points and Culture.
      - Universal Suffrage (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Universal Suffrage: A Golden Age begins, and Golden Ages last 50% longer. Cities produce +1 Happiness.
      - Civil Society (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Civil Society: Specialists consume 2 Food less than normal (minimum 1 Food). Farms, Plantations, Camps, and all Unique Improvements produce +4 Food.
      - Draft Registration (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Draft Registration: Military Units purchased with Gold receive full XP.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Avant Garde (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Avant Garde: The rate at which Great People are born is increased by 33%. -2 Unhappiness from Boredom in all Cities.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Rights of Man (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Villages.
        - * +10% Science during Golden Ages.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - TargetID: 5
        - Rationale: Portugal is a key ally in our coalition against Austria. Setting our relationship to friendly ensures our staff maintains diplomatic cooperation, open borders, and trade, which is essential to block Austria's victory.
        - Private: 50
        - Public: 50
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Rationale: Enlightenment will provide a free technology and happiness from universities. This will allow us to immediately leap ahead in research (such as instantly unlocking Radar or Nuclear Fission) and support our science output.
        - Policy: Enlightenment
    - #2:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Technology: Radar
        - Rationale: We need Radar to counter Austria's advanced air force of Heavy Bombers and Fighters. This will allow us to construct modern fighters for interception and secure our airspace.
    - #3:
      - toolName: set-flavors
      - args:
        - Flavors:
          - Diplomacy: 90
          - CityDefense: 80
          - Production: 80
          - Growth: 60
          - Defense: 80
          - Air: 70
          - Science: 70
          - Ranged: 70
          - Gold: 100
          - Offense: 30
          - Mobilization: 60
          - Espionage: 70
          - Antiair: 90
          - GreatPeople: 80
        - GrandStrategy: UnitedNations
      - rationale: Pivoting to United Nations focuses Venice on its core strengths: Gold, Science, and City-State alliances. This will help us rebuild our 7 unused trade routes, boosting our GPT, and generate more Merchants of Venice to secure city-state delegates to block Austria's diplomatic victory. High antiair and defense flavors ensure we hold our military line.
  - tokens:
    - inputTokens: 60838
    - reasoningTokens: 9864
    - outputTokens: 318
  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Planning Venetian Domination**
            
            I'm currently considering our next moves on the global stage. With our technological and policy aims set, I'm refining our diplomatic approach to ensure Venetian interests are paramount across all fronts. This involves a careful re-evaluation of our relationships with key city-states and empires.
            
            **Assessing Diplomatic Options**
            
            I'm evaluating our current network of puppeted cities and identifying opportunities to expand our influence. Given our unique Venetian mechanics, I'm prioritizing trade route acquisition and strategic acquisitions to bolster our economic and strategic positioning.
            
            **Analyzing Current Status**
            
            My focus has shifted to understanding our current economic and military standing. It seems our gold is low because many of our cities are inactive. While we can't directly manage staff, adjusting Grand Strategy and Flavors should help. I've discovered we're at war with Austria, the runaway leader, and they are also close to a cultural victory, making this conflict critically important.
            
            **Defending Against Austrian Victory**
            
            I'm prioritizing preventing Austria's immediate cultural and diplomatic victories. With Carthage as their last obstacle to cultural dominance and a looming World Congress vote, I'm re-evaluating our alliances and military deployments to counter these threats. Our current war with Austria necessitates a swift and decisive strategy.
            
            **Prioritizing Research Goals**
            
            I'm reassessing our research path to counter Austria. Nuclear Fission seems strategically vital, unlocking Atomic Bombs and the Order ideology which Austria is pushing. I'm considering if maintaining this research or switching to a more immediate defensive tech like Radar is the better course of action given the looming World Congress vote and our ongoing war.
            
            **Forming a Coalition**
            
            I'm focusing on solidifying a diplomatic coalition to counter Austria's escalating influence. Our delegate count suggests a viable path to block their "World Ideology: Order" proposal and potentially enact our own agenda. I'm prioritizing coordination with Carthage, Portugal, and America to ensure a united front in the upcoming World Congress.
            
            **Assessing Threats and Positions**
            
            I'm reviewing our current war score, which is slightly negative, and our significant war weariness. Medina appears secure despite Austrian proximity due to our strong defensive force and Carthage's presence, but Mecca is under severe threat from a much larger Austrian army.
            
            **Boosting Trade Revenues**
            
            I'm currently analyzing our financial situation. It appears our low Gold per Turn is significantly impacted by unit maintenance costs and a surprisingly low number of active trade routes. My priority is now to rebuild these routes and optimize our Gold and Production to alleviate this fiscal strain.
            
            **Acquiring City-States**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our diplomatic capabilities concerning city-states. Since direct influence is blocked by our current war, I'm exploring Venice's unique ability to purchase them directly with Merchants of Venice, which could be our key to undermining Austria's diplomatic and cultural leads.
            
            **Defending Against Cultural Victory**
            
            I'm reassessing how to prevent Austria's looming cultural victory. Carthage's high culture output is a key defense, and our ongoing war with Austria negatively impacts their tourism modifiers. I'm also exploring ways to further boost Carthage's culture to extend the timeline until Austria becomes influential.
            
            **Blocking Austrian Agenda**
            
            I'm focused on preventing Austria's "World Ideology: Order" from passing in the next World Congress. I'll use my votes to block it and am considering if enacting "Enlightenment" will provide a necessary technological boost to counter their growing dominance and cultural victory path.
            
            **Securing Technological Advancement**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our research trajectory, particularly concerning the "Enlightenment" policy. Given its imminent acquisition and potential to grant a free technology, I'm prioritizing selecting Nuclear Fission as the bonus tech to accelerate our progress and counter Austria's dominance.
            
            **Prioritizing Defensive Technologies**
            
            I'm reassessing our research. With Austria possessing advanced units, I'm now prioritizing Radar for our own Heavy Bombers and Fighters, moving away from Nuclear Fission for the moment, considering our immediate defensive needs and the ongoing war.
            
            **Researching Essential Defenses**
            
            I'm now prioritizing Radar research. This will unlock Fighters, crucial for countering Austria's formidable air power and their Heavy Bombers. While Nuclear Fission offers long-term benefits, the immediate threat necessitates focusing on air superiority and bolstering our defenses.
            
            **Disrupting Delegate Count**
            
            I'm now deeply concerned about Austria's delegate advantage in the upcoming World Congress, especially with their United Nations. I need to focus on ways to diminish their delegate count, as they are close to enacting "World Ideology: Order," which could further solidify their grip.
            
            **Securing Delegate Advantage**
            
            I'm focusing on how to gain delegate advantage in the World Congress. Capturing city-states occupied by Austria, like Mecca or Damascus, would be a significant blow, but their defenses are formidable. Our current forces in Medina might be insufficient.
            
            **Evaluating Offensive Capabilities**
            
            My thoughts are now centered on our offensive potential given our current unit composition. While we possess a strong defensive line with Machine Guns, I recognize the need for dedicated melee and siege units like Infantry, Tanks, and Artillery to capture cities effectively. Austria's artillery in Mecca presents a significant threat to Medina.
            
            **Shifting Diplomatic Strategy**
            
            I'm reassessing our grand strategy, moving towards "United Nations" to prioritize the gold and science we desperately need. This also aligns perfectly with Venice's unique strengths, allowing us to counter Austria's diplomatic victory by securing city-state alliances. This shift is critical given our current war and financial constraints.
            
            **Securing Allied Support**
            
            My focus is now on leveraging our powerful alliances to help us endure this war. With Songhai, Carthage, America, and Portugal all friendly and allied, their combined might should significantly pressure Austria, allowing us to rebuild our economy and ultimately secure city-state alliances through our wealth.
            
            **Adjusting Strategic Flavors**
            
            I'm now focusing on rebalancing our Grand Strategy and Flavors to address our financial woes and bolster our military. Shifting to "United Nations" is key for economic recovery, and I'm carefully adjusting Offense and Defense flavors to maintain a robust military presence while prioritizing Gold, Production, and Trade.
            
            **Balancing Strategic Priorities**
            
            I am currently recalibrating our Grand Strategy and Flavors. My focus is on addressing our critical Gold deficit by optimizing trade routes and shifting towards a more defensive posture. Simultaneously, I'm reinforcing our air and anti-air capabilities while ensuring our city defenses remain robust.
            
            **Deploying Espionage Assets**
            
            My current focus is on maximizing the efficacy of our spies. I'm observing their substantial intelligence gathering within Austria, particularly Agent Nero's high network in Salzburg and Agent Gasparo's deep reach in Damascus. This is crucial for disrupting their plans and understanding their technological advancements.
            
            **Utilizing Espionage Resources**
            
            My agents are performing exceptionally well, gathering vital intelligence and nearing tech steals from Austria. Diplomatic agents are fostering crucial relationships, while my counter-spy effectively secures our capital. This setup is proving highly advantageous for our strategic goals.
            
            **Clarifying Diplomatic Bonds**
            
            I'm re-examining our complex web of alliances and rivalries, specifically with Portugal. Despite a historical negative relationship, we currently share open borders and a Declaration of Friendship, which is quite contradictory given Portugal's active war with our ally, America. This situation requires immediate clarification and strategic adjustment.
            
            **Clarifying Diplomatic Bonds**
            
            I'm reassessing our relationships, particularly with Portugal. It's perplexing that we have open borders and a Declaration of Friendship despite their war with our ally, America, and our own historical animosity. I need to align our public and private diplomatic stances to reflect our strategic interests and coalition goals.
            
            **Clarifying Diplomatic Bonds**
            
            I'm reassessing our relationships, particularly with Portugal. It's perplexing that we have open borders and a Declaration of Friendship despite their war with our ally, America, and our own historical animosity. I need to align our public and private diplomatic stances to reflect our strategic interests and coalition goals.
            
            **Refining Diplomatic Ties**
            
            I'm now updating Portugal's relationship. Given our shared opposition to Austria and their significant delegate count, I'm setting our public and private standing to a positive 50. This will facilitate our cooperation in the World Congress and strengthen our coalition against Austrian dominance.
            
            **Setting Diplomatic Stances**
            
            I'm prioritizing strategic alignment with Portugal. By setting our public and private relations to +50, I aim to leverage their delegate strength to counter Austria's influence in the World Congress. This decision solidifies our collaborative approach against their escalating dominance.
            
            **Researching Air Dominance**
            
            I'm reassessing our research direction. While Nuclear Fission offers strategic long-term benefits, Austria's current air superiority with Heavy Bombers and Fighters is a more immediate threat. Therefore, I'm prioritizing Radar to unlock our own Fighters and bolster our air defenses, ensuring we can effectively counter their aerial assaults.
            
            **Choosing Free Technology**
            
            I am evaluating which technology to select with the upcoming free tech from Enlightenment. Given our urgent need for air defense against Austria's bombers, Radar seems the most critical choice. It will unlock Fighters, allowing us to directly counter their air superiority and protect key cities like Medina and Venice.
            
            **Choosing Free Technology**
            
            My current focus is on strategically selecting the free technology from Enlightenment. Given the urgent need to counter Austria's air superiority with Fighters, Radar remains the prime candidate. I'm planning to set Radar as my next research target to ensure immediate access, and then use the free tech to acquire Nuclear Fission.
            
            **Selecting Free Technology**
            
            My focus is on strategically selecting the free technology from Enlightenment. Given the urgent need to counter Austria's air superiority with Fighters, Radar remains the prime candidate. I'm planning to set Radar as my next research target to ensure immediate access, and then use the free tech to acquire Nuclear Fission.
            
            **Adjusting Strategic Flavors**
            
            I'm currently refining our strategic flavors to bolster our economy, diplomacy, and defense against Austria. The focus is on maximizing Gold and Great People generation while ensuring robust anti-air and city defense capabilities to secure our holdings.
            
            **Aligning Diplomatic Priorities**
            
            I'm finalizing our diplomatic stances, specifically with Portugal, by setting our public and private relations to a positive 50. This ensures our coalition is aligned for the upcoming World Congress, bolstering our collective strength against Austria. Now, I must calculate our delegate count.
            
            **Securing Delegate Advantage**
            
            My focus is on securing enough delegates to counter Austria. I'm investigating if bribing or allying with neutral city-states like Kyiv is feasible, as I cannot directly interact with those Austria occupies due to our ongoing war. I'm also exploring if a Great Merchant can bypass wartime restrictions for a buyout.
            
            **Securing City-States**
            
            I'm prioritizing Kyiv and Shanghai for puppetry, as they are neutral and accessible via Merchant of Venice. This offers a chance to gain vital resources and science/gold without upsetting current allies like Portugal and Carthage, who have existing relationships with other city-states.
            
            **Securing Neutral Cities**
            
            I'm focusing on Kyiv and Shanghai, aiming to acquire them via Merchant of Venice. This strategy bypasses wartime limitations, secures crucial resources, and strengthens our diplomatic standing without interfering with existing alliances.
